Volume - 157 BENGALURU, FRIDAY, 09, DECEMBER, 2022(MARGASHIRA, 18, SHAKAVARSHA, 1944) Issue 248

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
NO. LD 320 LSI 2021 Karnataka Government Secretariat,
Vikasa Soudha,
Bengaluru, dated:30-11-2022

NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (5) of section 1 of the Employees’ State
Insurance Act, 1948 (Central Act No: 34/1948) the Government of Karnataka in consultation with the
Employees’ State Insurance Corporation and with the approval of the Central Government vide letter No.
S 38025/07/2020 – SS-1, dated:09-06-2021, hereby gives notice of its intention to extend the provision of
the Act to the classes of establishment specified in the following schedule. The draft Notification No: LD
320 LSI 2021, dated:29-10-2022 was published in Part-4A, No:PR -774 in the Karnataka State Gazette dated:30-09
-2022.
A period has been fixed for receiving objections and suggestions from the classes of
establishment specified within one month of the publication of the said draft notification in the
Karnataka State Gazette and no objections and suggestions received during the said period.
In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (5) of section 1 of the Employees State
Insurance Act, 1948 (Central Act No: 34 of 1948), the Government of Karnataka hereby makes the
provisions of the Act applicable to the classes of establishments specified in the following Schedule.

(19370)

BENGALURU DATED: 21-11-2022

NOTIFICATION
Whereas the Draft notification to amend the Karnataka Employees State Insurance
Scheme (Medical) Services (Recruitment) Rules, 2007 was published as required by clause (a)
of section 3(2) of the Karnataka State Civil Services Act, 1978 (Karnataka Act 14 of 1990) In
part IV A of the Karnataka Gazette No.PR 787 dated:11-10-2022 inviting objections and
suggestions from all persons likely to be affected thereby within fifteen days from date of its
publication in the Officials’ Gazette.

And whereas any objections and suggestions has not been received from any
persons.

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1) and sub-
section (2) of section 3 of the Karnataka State Civil Services Act, 1978 (Karnataka Act 14 of
1990) read with section 8 the Government of Karnataka hereby makes the Following rules
namely: -

RULES
1. Title and commencement. - (1) these rules may be called the Karnataka Employees State
Insurance Scheme (Medical) Services (Recruitment) (Amendment) Rules, 2021.

(2) They Shall come into force on the date of their Publication in the Official
Gazette.

2. Amendment of Schedule. - in the Schedule to the Karnataka Employees State Insurance
Scheme (Medical) Services (Recruitment) Rules, 2007, in Serial No, 29 (a) in column (2) for the
brackets and figures “(10,000-18,150)” the brackets and figures “(8,000-14,800)”, Shall be deemed to
have been substituted with effect from 30.03.2012.

By order and in the name of the
Governor of Karnataka,

(Vijaya.N)
Desk Officer-5,
Labour Department
(ESI Scheme (M) Services)

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Bomlapura and Gudigondanahalli village of Tiptur Taluk of Tumakur District to
convert the existing Gollarahatti unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/ extention/part
of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is
specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Bomlapura and Gudigondanahalli village for conversion of Gollarahatti
unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/extention/part of the said revenue village.

Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act ,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into considaration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the state Government . Objections or suggetion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner , Tumakur district
SCHEDULE

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Gollarahatti village of Tiptur taluk of
Tumakur district and convert the same to form hamlet extention within the boundary
of Bomlapura and Gudigondanahalli Revenue village and same will here in after be
named as Gollarahatti of Bomlapura and Gudigondanahalli village Tiptur Taluk
Tumakur District

Hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the column(4) above with
survey numbers specified in column(3) on which the said unrecorded habitation is
located is proposed to be converted as habitation area ( Gramatana) of Gollarahatti
hamlet/extention/part of Bomlapura and Gudigondanahalli village in Tiptur Taluk
of Tumakur District

The proposal specified above shall come in to effect from the date of its final
notification to be published in the official gazette by the state Government under
section5 of the Karnatanka Land Revenue Act,1964(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964)

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the

Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Dannur and Nellur Villages of Aland taluk Kalaburagi District and to constitute a
new revenue village called Dannur Tanda and Gayamal Tanda sas specified in
schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Kalaburagi District, Kalaburagi.

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Dannur and Nellur of Aland taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new
revenue village called Dannur Tanda of Aland taluk Kalaburagi district along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 371 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangaluru , Date :08-12-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clause (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population , area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to alter the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Doddabeeranakere village of Turuvekere taluk Tumkur District to convert the
existing Danayakanapura and D B Hatti (Hamlet Village)unrecorded habitation in
to a hamlet/ extention/part of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by the
said hamlet of the village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Doddabeeranakere village for conversion of Danayakanapura and D B
Hatti (Hamlet Village) unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/extention/part of the said
revenue village.
Now therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the said Act ,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into considaration after thirty days from the
date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period
specified above will be considered by the state Government . Objections or suggetion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Tumkur district

specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Danayakanapura of Turuvekere taluk Tumkur District to from new
revenue village called as Doddabeeranakere of Turuvekere taluk Tumkur District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 372 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Stored Building
Bengaluru Date:08-12-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka land Revenue
Act,1964(Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as
the population, Area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience .The Government of Karnataka proposes issue a notification to alter the
limits of Hannikera village of Chitthapur Taluk Kalaburagi district to constitute a
new village called Bhojunayak Tanda, Ramanayak Tanda and Jairam Tanda as
specified in Schedule along with maps with old survey and new survey numbers
assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of tall the persons likely to be affected

thereby that the proposal will be taken consideration on or after thirty days from the
date of publication of the Notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government .Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner Kalaburagi District Kalaburagi.

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Hannikera of Chitthapur Taluk in Kalaburagi District to from new
revenue village called as Bhojunayak Tanda of Chitthapur Taluk and Kalaburagi
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1278
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 366 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:08-12-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv} of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of

Hulandgera Village of Chittapur Taluk of Kalaburagi District to convert the
existing Hulandgera Tanda sub village unrecorded habitation in to a
hamlet/extension /part of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said
hamlet of the village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Hulandgera village for conversion of Hulandgera Tanda sub village unrecorded
habitation in to hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government from
any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period specified
above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions may be
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburagi District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Hulandgera Tanda village of Chittapur
Taluk of Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the
boundary of Hulandgera Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Hulandgera Tanda of Hulandgera village in Chittapur Taluk of Kalaburagi
District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above
with survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation
is located is proposed to be converted as habitation area ( Gramatana) of Hulandgera
Tanda hamlet/ extension/part of Hulandgera village in Chittapur Taluk of
Kalaburagi District.

The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as the State
Government may specify by Notification proposed to be issued under section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964).

such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative

of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is
specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of

Korwar village for conversion of Ashoknagar Tanda sub village unrecorded
habitation in to hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburagi District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Ashoknagar Tanda village of Kalagi
Taluk of Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the
boundary of Korwar revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Ashoknagar Tanda of Korwar village in Kalagi Taluk of Kalaburagi District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Salahalli Village of Kalagi Taluk of Kalaburagi District to convert the existing
Salahalli Tanda sub village unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the
existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is
specified in the schedule below.

specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Salahalli village for conversion of Salahalli Tanda sub village unrecorded habitation
in to hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.

notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburagi District.
SCHEDULE

table shall be declared as habitation area of Salahalli Tanda village of Kalagi Taluk
of Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 303 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:05-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 303 PFH 2022 Dated:12-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Maratur village of Shahabad Taluk and
Kalaburagi District and constituting a new revenue village called Martur Station
Tanda was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 303 PFH 2022
dated:13-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:13-10-
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Maratur of Shahabad taluk in Kalburagi District to form a new revenue
village called Martur Station Tanda of Shahabad taluk Kalburagi district along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1289
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 306 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:05-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 306 PFH 2022 Dated:.11-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Mudnal village of Yadagiri Taluk and
Yadagiri District and constituting a new revenue village called Umlanayaknagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 306 PFH 2022
dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated dated:14-
10-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Mudnal of Yadagiri taluk in Yadagiri District to form a new revenue village
called Umlanayaknagar of Yadagiri taluk Yadagiri district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 224 PFH 2022 Dated:. 11-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Peta Ammapur village of Shorapur Taluk
and Yadgir District and constituting a new revenue village called Ammapur was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 224 PFH 2022
dated:13-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:13-
10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Peta Ammapur of Shorapur taluk in Yadagiri District to form a new
revenue village called Ammapur of Shorapur taluk Yadagiri district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the notification No.RD 24 GLH 2022 Dated:
09-02-2022 regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of
Raygonahalli village of Kunigal Taluk in Tumkur District for constituting
hamlet/extention called Kalasipalya within the boundary of Raygonahalli revenue
village was published as required by section 6 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No.PR -151 Dated:14-02-
2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected
thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on Date:14-02-
2022.

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Goverenmen of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extention
wihin the limits of Raygonahalli revenue village, namely:- Kalasipalya
SCHEDULE

Lands bearig survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Raygonahalli village of Kunigal Taluk of
Tumkuru District and convert the same to form hamlet/extention within the boundary
of Raygonahalli Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Kalasipalya
village kunigal Taluk of Tumkuru District.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 177 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 177 GLH 2022 Dated:12-
10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Honagunta Village of Shahabad taluk
Kalaburgi District and constituting a sub village called Vaddarwadi was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 177 GLH 2022 dated:13-10-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 13-
10-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Honagunta of Shahabad taluk in Kalaburgi District to form a new revenue
village called Vaddarwadi of Shahabad taluk Kalaburgi district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1295

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 228 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:05-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 228 PFH 2022 Dated:11-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Yalagi village of Shorapur Taluk and Yadgir
District and constituting a new revenue village called Shivajinagar was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 228 PFH 2022 dated:13-10-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
dated:13-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Yalagi of Shorapur taluk in Yadagiri District to form a new revenue village
called Shivajinagar of Shorapur taluk Yadagiri district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 202 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date: 05-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 202 GLH 2022 Dated:10-
10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Yelleri Village of Gurumatkal taluk Yadgiri
District and constituting a village called Ashapurnagar and J. B. Nagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 202 GLH 2022
dated:12-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 12-
10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Yelleri of Gurumatkal taluk in Yadgiri District to form a new revenue village
called Ashapurnagar of Gurumatkal taluk Yadgiri district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) there of.

PR-1297
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 273 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:06-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 273 PFH 2022 Dated:13-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Bachanal Village of Kamalapur Taluk of
Kalaburagi District and constituting a sub village called Bachanal Nagar, Sulagutti
Nagar and Ratanagutti Nagar was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka
Gazette No. RD 273 PFH 2022 dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 14-
10-2022.

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:
SCHEDULE 1
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Bachanal Nagar village of Kamalapur
Taluk of Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the
boundary of Bachanal Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Bachanal Nagar of Bachanal village in Kamalapur Taluk of Kalaburagi District.

SCHEDULE 2
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Sulagutti Nagar village of Kamalapur
Taluk of Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the
boundary of Bachanal Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Sulagutti Nagar of Bachanal village in Kamalapur Taluk of Kalaburagi District.

village called Ratanagutti Nagar of Kamalapur taluk Kalaburagi district along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 179 GLH 2022 Dated:12-

Kalaburgi District and constituting a new revenue village called Belur(J) Tanda was

(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 179 GLH 2022
dated:15-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated15-10-

here by makes the following notification, namely:-

specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said

village called Belur(J) Tanda of Kalaburgi taluk Kalaburgi district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 262 PFH 2022 Dated:11-10-

District and constituting a sub village called Chatrunayaknagar and
Khirunayaknagar was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD
262 PFH 2022 dated:12-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 12-
10-2022.

the State Government;

Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Navnihal of Kamalapur taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue
village called Charunayaknagar of Kamalapur taluk Kalaburagi district along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 272 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:06-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 272 PFH 2022 Dated:13-10-2022
regarding altering the limits of Gogi Village of Kamalapur taluk Kalaburagi
District and constituting a village called Purunayak nagar was published as required
by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964)
in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 272 PFH 2022 dated:14-10-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 14-10-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Gogi of Kamalapur taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue
village called Purunayak nagar of Kamalapur taluk Kalaburagi district along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

Multi storied Building
Bangalore , Date:06-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 280 PFH 2022 Dated:13-10-2022
regarding altering the limits of Kalamud with its hamlets of Kamalapur Taluk
Kalaburagi District and constituting a village called Namanayak nagar,
Monunayak nagar and Limbunayak nagar was published as required by section 6
of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the
Karanataka Gazette No. RD 280 PFH 2022 dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 14-10-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:
SCHEDULE -1

Lands bearing Survey Numbers of the villages and Survey Numbers of the
Areas as specified in column(2) of the table below shall be excluded from limits of
said Kalamud Village Kamalapur Taluk Kalaburagi district to form a new
revenue village called as Namanayak nagar of Kamalapur Taluk Kalaburagi
district along with Survey Number included in Column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 267 PFH 2022 Dated:11-10-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Maramanchi of Kamalapura taluk Kalaburagi
District and constituting a village called Gopinayaknagar was published as required
by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964)
in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 267 PFH 2022 dated:12-10-2022 inviting

objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 12-
10-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Maramanchi of Kamalapura taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new
revenue village called Gopinayaknagar of Kamalapura taluk Kalaburagi district
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:08-12-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 178 GLH 2022 Dated:12-
10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Minajagi of Kalaburagi taluk in Kalaburagi
District and constituting a sub village called Minajagi Tanda was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 178 GLH 2022 dated:14-10-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated: 14-
10-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Minajagi of Kalaburagi taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue
village called Minajagi Tanda of Kalaburagi taluk Kalaburagi district along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.
